- **athena-partition-projection-advisory**: Finds Amazon Athena and Glue tables that scan far more S3 data than needed - unpartitioned tables over log-shaped prefixes correlated with recent high-ProcessedBytes queries - and emits ready-to-run partition-projection DDL, an optional partitioned-CTAS reorg, and a Parquet-conversion recommendation, with a projected bytes-scanned reduction. Use this skill for the automated partition-projection-DDL angle, distinct from general query tuning and scan-limit governance. Read-only and recommend-only. ([Misterb91123/aws-sample-ai-agents](https://github.com/Misterb91123/aws-sample-ai-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/data-analytics/athena-partition-projection-advisory))
- **athena-point**: Athena 流程評分與分流器。接收 PM 需求單、bug 描述或一句行為敘述後， 先用客觀 rubric 評估複雜度、風險、知識依賴與影響範圍，再決定任務應直接進入 build，或必須先走 spec/plan。當使用者說「/point」「評分」「這個要不要走 spec」、 「這只是小 bug 嗎」時觸發。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-point))

- **athena-pr-reviewer-workflow**: PROACTIVELY USED when reviewing a PR, branch, or Jira story. Runs a multi-reviewer code review in a background workflow (7 parallel Opus specialists → Sonnet batched verifiers → Sonnet synthesis), keeping reviewer agents out of main-thread context and returning a synthesized themed report. Triggers on "review PR X", "review this branch", "review CSD-123", "workflow review", "background review", or "/athena-workflow". - **athena-query-failure-troubleshooter**: Diagnoses Amazon Athena query failures through a thirteen-category diagnostic tree: S3 bucket access denied (Glue Data Catalog vs S3 data bucket permissions), SerDe mismatch (OpenCSVSerDe vs LazySimpleSerDe vs ParquetHiveSerDe), column type mismatch (STRING vs INT vs DOUBLE), partition projection errors, incorrect table location, stale Glue Catalog partitions (MSCK REPAIR), CTAS (CREATE TABLE AS) destination bucket permissions, workgroup output location, service query timeout (30-min limit), data format inference errors, SerDe property misconfiguration (quoteChar, escapeChar, separatorChar),… ([jhkchan/aws-agent-skills](https://github.com/jhkchan/aws-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-query-failure-troubleshooter) · ★4)
- **athena-query-helper**: Write and debug Athena SQL against the Kiro user_report and legacy by_user_analytic tables, including when to bypass Athena for S3 direct reads. Use when adding API routes or working on the data layer. ([whchoi98/kiro-dashboard](https://github.com/whchoi98/kiro-dashboard/tree/HEAD/.kiro/skills/athena-query-helper) · ★14)
- **athena-query-optimizer**: Optimizes Amazon Athena query performance and cost via a layered analysis framework covering partitioning (partition projection, dynamic partition pruning), file format selection (Parquet vs CSV vs JSON — Parquet is 10-100x faster via columnar pruning), compression (Snappy vs GZIP vs ZSTD), bucketing for JOIN optimization, workgroup settings (data scanned limits, query timeout), CTAS (CREATE TABLE AS SELECT for materialization), query rewriting (avoid SELECT *, enforce partition filters, use APPROXIMATE functions), and cost modeling ($5/TB scanned). Supports Athena query result reuse, Athena… ([jhkchan/aws-agent-skills](https://github.com/jhkchan/aws-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-query-optimizer) · ★4)
- **athena-query-performance-optimizer**: Optimizes Amazon Athena query performance and cost by analyzing data scanned per query, partition pruning, file formats, small-file overhead, partition projection, result caching, and workgroup controls. Use this skill when Athena queries are slow, scan large volumes of data, or cost more than expected under the per-terabyte scanned pricing model. ([Misterb91123/aws-sample-ai-agents](https://github.com/Misterb91123/aws-sample-ai-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/data-analytics/athena-query-performance-optimizer))

- **atk-link-checker**: 1. **逐条核验，禁止盲修**：每个疑似问题都必须查看所在文件、行号、上下文和目标候选，不能只靠脚本结果批量替换。 2. **先判断是否真错**：VuePress 支持多种合法链接形式，不能因为普通文件路径解析失败就认定为坏链。 3. **只修确定性问题**：只有目标唯一、语义匹配、链接形式明确错误时才修改。 4. **不确定就不改**：无法确认正确目标的链接保留原样，最终只报告这些链接并给出建议。 5. **仅手动触发**：此技能不根据上下文自动激活。 6. **不主动构建**：完成修复后不要运行 `npm run build:standalone`，用户自行验证。 7. **最终报告保持精简**：最终只告诉用户仍不确定、需要用户确认的链接，不输出已修复清单或无问题统计。 ([xgMeow/atk_doc_dev](https://github.com/xgMeow/atk_doc_dev/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/atk-link-checker) · 2 copies)

- **atlas-esl-hop**: ESL lifecycle hop — the DISCOVER hop. When a scout mission's findings surface change-worthy signal (a defect, a spec/impl drift, or a genuine gap) in an ESL-enabled project (.spectra/ present), ATLAS proposes opening an ESL change at 'proposed' by framing the scout-report + its ECL envelope it already emits as the proposal artifact — it never calls mcp__tonberry__* itself and never implements (refusal boundary intact). SPECTRA's own esl-hop owns the actual right_size/propose/specify tonberry calls on receipt, over the existing atlas-to-spectra handoff edge.
